Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any ocean module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 364. The projective resolution has length at most 21 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to ocean theory, we decompose the problem into 364 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 21. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
